# Kimigayo Compiler Plan

Current plan only: position, milestone order, completion conditions, next actions and open issues. Update when position, next actions or blockers change, under [AGENTS Documentation](../../AGENTS.md#documentation). Required behavior is in [SPEC.md](../SPEC.md); implemented support is in [STATUS.md](../STATUS.md). Commits and artifacts hold unit evidence; [PLAN_HISTORY.md](PLAN_HISTORY.md) preserves selected historical summaries and is maintained only on explicit request. Items deferred by [Appendix D](../spec/appendices/D-deferred-features.md) are not planned here.

## 1. Scope

Implement the finalized language of SPEC.md (Chapters 1–19 and 22) and the implementation contracts of [IMPL.md](../IMPL.md) (Chapters 20–21, the test execution profile and Appendix A) for the Windows x64 profile, excluding what Appendix D defers; ObjectCallCompatible stage 2 (Appendix D.2) is in scope. Work follows unfinished implementation items and their dependencies. Milestone Programs require explicit user instruction as implementation targets; existing program results remain evidence, not stopping conditions.

- **Generics:** the initial profile monomorphizes (§21.3.1). Generic code sharing is deferred.
- **Origins and the Contract/type system** (§8, §15) remain complete requirements; implementation limits never narrow them.
- **Async tasks and workers:** implementation is deferred by user instruction; the preserved plan is Appendix D. Remaining callable effect-bound integration is G78.
- **Adopted library contracts:** channel delivery is FIFO by successful-send commit order, and remaining items are completely destroyed one at a time in reverse order (SPEC §22.3.4). Apply these when the deferred async implementation resumes. Slice `firstIndexWhere(predicate:)` remains unimplemented within the existing library support boundary.
- **Proposal text changes** and **NativeAOT tests** need explicit instruction. Instructed intake or closure includes register updates and moves under AGENTS.

## 2. Working rules

Work policy (commit and push per unit, a final Session per working session) is in [AGENTS.md](../../AGENTS.md); design and review in [WORKFLOW.md](WORKFLOW.md); checks and evidence in [VERIFICATION.md](VERIFICATION.md); structure rules, migration rules and the stage ledger in [COMPILER_ARCHITECTURE.md](COMPILER_ARCHITECTURE.md). PLAN holds current work and keeps explicit blockers with their next steps. A closed §7 row is deleted, its one-line summary kept in the landing commit; [PLAN_HISTORY](PLAN_HISTORY.md#closed-open-issues) changes only on an explicit request (AGENTS' update table; the owner, 2026-11-13, superseding 2026-11-01's 「完了済みの行を PLAN_HISTORY に要約して移す」); its open remainder stays here.

IDs follow the adopted implementation progression; [former IDs](../../tests/milestones/README.md#implementation-order) identify pre-2026-10-15 evidence. ### Shared object track (alternating sessions)

rc/arc for P40 and the nonnull Option representation before P41. Design and the mode-opening rule (`SupportsType`): §3 of [draft/Changes/2026-10-03 Shared Object Ownership Plan.md](../../draft/Changes/2026-10-03%20Shared%20Object%20Ownership%20Plan.md).

| ID | Unit | State | Completion condition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| U7 | P40 completion: `test-milestone40.ps1`, `tests/milestones/README.md`, stage baseline, STATUS, CODEMAP, `src/Benchmark/SharedObjects.md` | IN_PROGRESS | Remaining before §5, the 2026-10-09 audit's remainder after U7-1–U7-7 (`bdc55ed8`–`404136bf`; forms, sites and probes: `P34_REMAINING.md` at 4fb6fcdb1), by root cause in serial units, known wrong code first. **R1** Selection does not model the object layer reached through reference layers (SPEC 3.4.3, 7.3, 13.5.5): inherited getters and `ref/Self` methods through `ref`/`uniq` layers read the wrong address (wrong code under the old generator; MIR code generation reads the base subobject, R5-M114, R5-M158, audit probes not re-run); a custom setter or compound assignment through `uniq/(rc|arc/T)` reports `InternalInvariant_Kd`, a Field write there `SharedPathAccess_Kd`; direct setters on rc/arc lack the shared-authority Note; implicit receivers at a held handle (`slot.read()`, `slot.twice`) report Language codes; Field reads through `ref/(rc/T)` and value-call callees through a reference or element are Unsupported. Root fix: one selection model (all ref/uniq layers, then one object layer, with the nearest shared layer) used by Binding authority, receiver adaptation and Ownership; it replaces the `IndirectObjectCallee` limit. **R2** In-place operand consumers bypass the common Place route (SPEC 13.4, 13.5.5): `let r = s@ref; if r == take(s@move)` reads freed memory (the comparison is no use of its operands); followed payloads (`show(h@follow@ref)`, `h@follow == "abc"`, `g@follow[1]`, `a@follow.id`, `a.name == "n"`) report Language codes. Root fix: the comparison uses both operand Places (no Read op); followed Places take the common borrow route at every in-place consumer. **R3** Temporary Loan roots are an allow-list: generic temporaries are no roots (`keep<T>(make: () -> T)`, SPEC 8.15, STATUS); one shared predicate. **R4** obj payload content writes record no Loan on the handle (`local.value = m@ref`; `local@follow.value = m@ref` is Unsupported); `o@follow[0] = 7` on `[4, 5]@obj` is `InvalidAssignment_Kd`. Exclusive-object semantics belong to P39. For P40 completion, at least report both Origin-carrying spellings as one located Unsupported and fix the element-write category; recording Loans on the handle is the root fix. **R5** A pair-Semantics callee `v()` is `NotCallable_Kd` (`v@follow()` with `ref/s/F`: `UnresolvedBinding_Kd`); a Consuming `h@follow()` of a Copy closure is Unsupported. **Other:** `-> ref/i32 during handle.source` through an owning handle is `InvalidOriginBinding_Kd`; untested audit rows (creation forms, objuniq/upcast and `during` on rc/arc, no implicit payload follow at fixed Types, object equality vs value equality, owning receivers through rc/arc, release order, destructor Abort, payload projections without Take); outside P40, to file: a borrow of a Moved Place adds `ComparisonLoanConflict_Kd`, implicit `raw/F` calls need no unsafe context, `Kimi.Weak<rc/T>` is `UnresolvedBinding_Kd`, the omitted-base Unsupported lacks evidence, exclusive base receivers through obj (OCC-X), a Never-typed `let` initializer reports `InternalInvariant_Kd`. Then the README P40 row, PLAN, STATUS, CODEMAP and `SharedObjects.md`, a Session with `-Milestone ((1..37) + 40)`, `--object-plans` and `benchmark-object-finalization.ps1` alone. |

## 5. Completion conditions (every program milestone)

1. The spec-derived program source builds unchanged with the selected compiler configuration (Release by default; Debug explicitly) and runs at O0 and O2 with the expected stdout, exit code and stderr.
2. `src/backend/windows-x64/test-milestone<N>.ps1` exists and passes: original source only, one build/direct run per O0/O2; dedicated feature tests cover behavior and required rejection diagnostics.
3. Session verification passes: zero warnings, the full suite for the selected compiler configuration, and the harnesses of all earlier completed programs.
4. The owning SPEC sections exercised by the program have focused positive and negative tests; unsupported neighboring forms fail with a diagnostic, never with wrong code.
5. STATUS.md describes the new support boundary, and the milestone is marked DONE here and in `tests/milestones/README.md`.

A program source is re-spelled only for a specification change or for an authoring correction, where the source contradicts its own specification-derived output. `tests/milestones/README.md` records each re-spelling with its cause; a completed program keeps DONE when conditions 1–3 hold for the re-spelled source.

Features that a program's source does not use belong to the milestone that owns them, not to that program.

## 6. Next actions

1. **Alternate timed targets (after R6; until then only wrong-code fixes):** the U7 remainder (§4's U7 row), then the P38 completion review (user decision). Exclusive receiver proofs follow P42 → OCC-X. Retain Unsupported guards until the common plan passes analysis and execution; continue independent work when blocked.
2. **Callable and collections:** complete G10/G65 remainders and the G75 residual boundaries. Keep ranking independent of bodies, erasure incomparable with other adaptations and selected failures without fallback. Library `first`/`last` and Dictionary pair access remain separate from completed P36/P34.
3. **Verification and diagnostics:** migrate raw allocation measurements only when touched or recurring, preserving counts/order and failures. Carry earlier Move/Loan evidence and distinguish condition/Place identities. Review [inherited audit findings](DIAGNOSTICS.md#11-audit-findings-d1), including Note omissions and range-specific cases; no old audit item is closed merely by this documentation cleanup. Manual VS Code Quick Fix inspection and malformed-attribute recovery remain open.
4. **Effects:** generic-call summaries and caller static-Loan comparisons remain G78/STATUS limits. Static storage and shutdown belong to P42.
5. **Objects and FFI:** U8 implements nonnull handle Options before P41; T2 retains inferred C-layout diagnostics. P39 owns refinement and obj/objuniq invariance; P41 requires the Weak variance exception; P42 owns two-phase shutdown, static Abort codes and static Place roots before OCC-X/P43.
6. **Code rules and small context:** after analyzer retirement (§3; DECISIONS CR-R1), retain structural/size review obligations, ArchitectureRulesTest and entry-file contracts (AGENTS, DECISIONS Q1).

Concurrent sessions use separate worktrees and stage only their own paths. Library bodies reached only through generic dispatch are collected through the instance collector's Witness targets (`InstanceCollector.Callees`, `TyCtxt.Witness`; InstanceCollectorTest's K 4.5 rows); retain that path when selecting verification.

| G650 | Cold allocation scaling series and whole-suite load. Cause (A), the code tier, is fixed (`claude/f-g650` and `claude/f-g650-cost`: the test process runs without quick JIT, and `MeasureSeries` refuses outside `AllocationIsolationCollection`, where every cold series runs). G507's `ParserRegressionTest.ALongLeadingRegionParsesInLinearAllocation` measured a cold series by three `MeasureOnce` calls in the ordinary collection and failed a loaded whole-suite Session (158,440 / 312,304 / 718,568 B); FLK (`claude/f-flk`) moved it into `ParserRegressionTest.AllocationTests` through `MeasureSeries`, the bound unchanged. Remaining: a staged removal (AGENTS "Separate commits"): `AllocationMeasurement.Measure`'s default-comparer excuse (`CompileEvents`, the JIT-event listener and the invalid-window rule) explains a tier-0 first run that the test process no longer makes; a Front unit removes it once a whole-suite Session with the excuse turned into a failure passes (no window needs it). Also: `MeasureHops` states the same isolation requirement by comment and fails late; it takes `MeasureSeries`' refusal (one requirement, one check). The bounds are unchanged. (W-100 try 1; D-146; F's rulings.) Also under a loaded whole-suite Session (three heavy runs at once, 978 s of tests; G759's refresh, 11-12 17:2x, evidence `bin\worktrees\f-g759i\artifacts\verify\20261112-082108-521-session-refresh-g759`): `InferenceSlotScalingTest+AllocationTests.AColdBindOfCallsIsLinear("joins")` measured 7,064,392 / 7,411,208 / 9,586,056 B although it runs in `AllocationIsolationCollection` through `MeasureSeries`; it passes alone 3 of 3 on the same build. The cause (G650's triage, 2026-11-12): the measuring thread's pooled buffers, emptied by a High-pressure `ArrayPool<T>.Shared` trim (G650 (B)); every recorded recurrence is one `ArrayPool<Walked>` chain, the 11-11 one included. Fixed (`claude/f-g650b`, the owner's O-218 (a)): each run of a cold series, the unmeasured one too, runs on a new compiler thread, whose own pooled buffers are empty (DECISIONS R5-M172's amending line); no bound changed. | Front (test infrastructure); after R7's exit (plan revision 69 (B)) |

Decide per site (measure, avoid the copy, or accept), whether to report it upstream, and whether RP8 rows on this host need a note (a copied struct growing past 24 bytes can move any RP8 measurement). The transition needs ReadyToRun framework code beside JIT VEX code, which `DOTNET_TieredCompilation=0` keeps for the whole run (G753's matrix, 2026-11-12: R9-M1 part 3's fenced-code slowdown, 6.8-10x under TC=0, is absent under default tiering, with `DOTNET_ReadyToRun=0` and with `DOTNET_EnableAVX=0`; warm Generate on Milestone26 is about 1.2x slower under TC=0 than in the other two TC=0 variants. The product is NativeAOT, not measured. T-155 (2026-11-12) found configuration (B), TC=0 plus `DOTNET_ReadyToRun=0`, worse on the Bind columns, so compiler units keep (A) (O-189 (b)); the owner's O-207 (a) times R9-M1 part 3 under (B) (T-167: PASS). | G778 | Pre-existing (G647's design revision 2, section 7; profile `front\G647\r2\profile-Plt-100-V-cli-b.txt`, counters `front\G647\r2\retained-b.txt`): a function whose signature nests n layers with a named Origin on each compiles in time growing as about n^4. Dev ddfac15eb at 33, 65, 100 and 129 layers, one thread: 3, 4, 10 and 40 s for a returned `ref` chain (`Odeep`), 2, 4, 20 and 99 s for a comparison through pair layers (`Plt`), 88 s at 129 through a call (`Ocall`); every `ref` form takes 55 to 75 s at 255; after G647, `check` of a pair-layer program takes 1.7, 3.0 and about 41 s at 33, 65 and 129 layers (the Middle's f4, valid and invalid twins alike; on dev every pair-layer family exceeds 300 s at 255 to 300 layers). Cause, two parts of one path: (a) n nested Origins give n(n-1)/2 outlives premises and one proof request per pair, and each request closes over the whole premise graph again (requests x edges: 4.06 million edge visits at 64 layers, 47 million for two such Types and a result at 68); (b) the Origin premise environment is extracted again for each request (`ExtractOriginPremises` from `PublishedPremise`), `AddTypePremises` walks the input Type and at each layer `StoredPremisePart` walks the Type below it, and each stored part of a pair layer resolves the use's constraint scope by a walk up the syntax (`PairSlotCondition` and `PairBinders` call `ConstraintScope(Koto)`: 74% of the compile thread at 100 pair layers; 6.9 s against 0.5 s for `ref` layers at 64 with equal counters). G647's tests are sized for it (in process, Release: a pair-layer program at 65 layers 5 to 8 s, an Origin relation through a call at 67 layers 15 s). Proposed: (1) `PairSlotCondition` and `PairBinders` take the environment's scope (`environment.Scope`, already computed); (2) one walk of a Type yields the stored premises of all its layers; (3) the environment and the closure of a signature's premise graph are built once per signature and scope and shared by its requests. Closed when the summed edge work of the nested-layer family (`OriginProofScalingTest.NestedLayerRelationsFollowTheOriginPairs`, G647) grows with the premises (at most four times from 34 to 68 layers; today 16 times) and a timing row shows 129 layers within four times 65 for the `Plt` and `Ocall` forms. | Front (Binding); R7 plan: R7-C8 (plan revision 69 (B)) |
